Ey_at_ijk_Hx) / dz\n        dEz_dy_Hx = (Ez_at_ijp1k - Ez_at_ijk_Hx) / dy\n\n        mu_Hx = mu_xx[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hx = -dt / (mu_Hx + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HX] += ch_Hx * (dEy_dz_Hx - dEz_dy_Hx)\n\n        # ---- Hy component update ----\n        # Hy at (i, j, k) uses Ez at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dEz/dx\n        # and Ex at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dEx/dz\n        Ez_at_ijk_Hy = E[i, j, k, _EZ]\n        Ez_at_ip1jk = E[i + 1, j, k, _EZ]\n        Ex_at_ijk_Hy = E[i, j, k, _EX]\n        Ex_at_ijkp1 = E[i, j, k + 1, _EX]\n\n        dEz_dx_Hy = (Ez_at_ip1jk - Ez_at_ijk_Hy) / dx\n        dEx_dz_Hy = (Ex_at_ijkp1 - Ex_at_ijk_Hy) / dz\n\n        mu_Hy = mu_yy[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hy = -dt / (mu_Hy + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HY] += ch_Hy * (dEz_dx_Hy - dEx_dz_Hy)\n\n        # ---- Hz component update ----\n        # Hz at (i, j, k) uses Ex at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dEx/dy\n        # and Ey at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dEy/dx\n        Ex_at_ijk_Hz = E[i, j, k, _EX]\n        Ex_at_ijp1k = E[i, j + 1, k, _EX]\n        Ey_at_ijk_Hz = E[i, j, k, _EY]\n        Ey_at_ip1jk = E[i + 1, j, k, _EY]\n\n        dEx_dy_Hz = (Ex_at_ijp1k - Ex_at_ijk_Hz) / dy\n        dEy_dx_Hz = (Ey_at_ip1jk - Ey_at_ijk_Hz) / dx\n\n        mu_Hz = mu_zz[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hz = -dt / (mu_Hz + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HZ] += ch_Hz * (dEx_dy_Hz - dEy_dx_Hz)\n\nelse:\n    # Stub definitions when Warp is unavailable\n    electric_update_3d = None\n    magnetic_update_3d = None","originalFile":"\"\"\"Core Maxwell timestepping kernels for Phase 1.\n\nThis module provides the GPU-accelerated E and H field update kernels.\nThe kernels follow the Warp backend conventions documented in ``backend.py``.\n\nKernel Organization\n------------------\nThe Maxwell update is split into explicit stages:\n\n1. **boundary_exchange** — fill ghost cells before each update\n2. **electric_update** — update electric field components\n3. **source_injection** — add source contributions to E\n4. **boundary_exchange** — re-exchange E ghosts before H update\n5. **magnetic_update** — update magnetic field components\n6. **pml_stage** — apply PML/ABC damping (post-H update)\n7. **convergence_check** — integrated field intensity (Python-side)\n\nUpdate Equations\n---------------\nFor a Yee lattice with cell sizes ``(dx, dy, dz)`` and timestep ``dt``:\n\nElectric field update::\n\n    Ex[i+1] = Ex[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hz[i,j+1,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dy - (Hy[i,j,k+1] - Hy[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Ey[i+1] = Ey[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hx[i,j,k+1] - Hx[i,j,k])/dz - (Hz[i+1,j,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Ez[i+1] = Ez[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hy[i+1,j,k] - Hy[i,j,k])/dx - (Hx[i,j+1,k] - Hx[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nMagnetic field update::\n\n    Hx[i+1] = Hx[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ez[i,j+1,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dy - (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Hy[i+1] = Hy[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ex[i,j,k+1] - Ex[i,j,k])/dz - (Ez[i+1,j,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Hz[i+1] = Hz[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ey[i+1,j,k] - Ey[i,j,k])/dx - (Ex[i,j+1,k] - Ex[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nCoefficients\n-----------\nFor efficiency, the coefficients are precomputed as::\n\n    ce_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for E update\n    ce_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for H update (note the sign)\n    cm_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for magnetic current (same as ce_e)\n    cm_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for magnetic update (same as ce_h)\n\nIn a homogeneous medium these are constants. In an inhomogeneous medium\nthey vary spatially and are sampled from coefficient arrays.\n\nCapture-Safe Stepping\n---------------------\nAll timestep-dependent values are passed as explicit kernel arguments:\n\n- ``dt``: scalar float timestep\n- ``step_index``: int step counter (for time-varying sources)\n- ``time``: float current simulation time\n\nSource amplitudes are computed in Python and passed as scalar or array\narguments. No closure captures are used in kernel definitions.\n\nReferences\n---------\n- Vacuum update: ``../meep/src/update_eh.cpp``\n- Kernel formula sources: ``../phase1/kernel-formula-sources.md``\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any, Literal\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom autofdtd.kernels.backend import (\n    WARP_AVAILABLE,\n    ComplexFieldPolicy,\n    WarpTimer,\n    allocate_coefficient_array,\n    allocate_field_array,\n    backend_info,\n    nvtx_range,\n    step_metrics,\n    wp,\n)\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"electric_update_3d\",\n    \"magnetic_update_3d\",\n    \"MaxwellStepKernelSpec\",\n    \"allocate_maxwell_arrays\",\n    \"step_maxwell\",\n    \"step_kernel_metadata\",\n]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kernel metadata\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass MaxwellStepKernelSpec:\n    \"\"\"Metadata for a Maxwell timestep kernel.\n\n    Attributes\n    ----------\n    name : str\n        Kernel function name.\n    field_family : str\n        \"electric\" or \"magnetic\".\n    uses_pml : bool\n        Whether this kernel requires PML coefficient arrays.\n    uses_sources : bool\n        Whether this kernel has source injection integrated.\n    supports_complex : bool\n        Whether this kernel supports complex-valued fields.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str\n    field_family: Literal[\"electric\", \"magnetic\"]\n    uses_pml: bool = False\n    uses_sources: bool = False\n    supports_complex: bool = True\n\n\ndef step_kernel_metadata() -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Expose the current stepping kernel metadata for diagnostics.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"backend\": \"warp\" if WARP_AVAILABLE else \"numpy\",\n        \"warp_available\": WARP_AVAILABLE,\n        \"module_contents_stable\": False,  # inv_dz\n    dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n\n    ce_Ey = dt / (eps_yy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 1] += ce_Ey * (dHx_dz_Ey - dHz_dx_Ey)\n\n    # ---- Ez component update ----\n    # Ez: dHy/dx - dHx/dy\n    dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n    dHx_dy_Ez = (Hx[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n\n    ce_Ez = dt / (eps_zz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 2] += ce_Ez * (dHy_dx_Ez - dHx_dy_Ez)\n\n\ndef numpy_magnetic_update_3d(\n    H: np.ndarray,\n    E: np.ndarray,\n    mu_xx: np.ndarray,\n    mu_yy: np.ndarray,\n    mu_zz: np.ndarray,\n    dt: float,\n    dx: float,\n    dy: float,\n    dz: float,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"NumPy implementation of 3D magnetic field update.\n\n    Updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the curl of E.\n    Ghost cells (index 0 and -1 on each axis) are not updated.\n\n    Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n    component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n    Args:\n        H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        mu_xx, mu_yy, mu_zz: Permeability arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz).\n        dt: Timestep size.\n        dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes.\n    \"\"\"\n    nx, ny, nz = H.shape[:3]\n    # Interior indices: [1, nx-2], [1, ny-2], [1, nz-2]\n    i1, i2 = 1, nx - 1\n    j1, j2 = 1, ny - 1\n    k1, k2 = 1, nz - 1\n\n    # Precompute 1/dy, 1/dz, 1/dx for efficiency\n    inv_dy = 1.0 / dy\n    inv_dz = 1.0 / dz\n    inv_dx = 1.0 / dx\n\n    # ---- Hx component update ----\n    # Hx: dEy/dz - dEz/dy\n    Ey = E[:, :, :, 1]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n    Ez = E[:, :, :, 2]\n\n    dEy_dz_Hx = (Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n    dEz_dy_Hx = (Ez[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Ez[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n\n    ch_Hx = -dt / (mu_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 0] += ch_Hx * (dEy_dz_Hx - dEz_dy_Hx)\n\n    # ---- Hy component update ----\n    # Hy: dEz/dx - dEx/dz\n    Ex = E[:, :, :, 0]\n\n    dEz_dx_Hy = (Ez[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Ez[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n    dEx_dz_Hy = (Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n\n    ch_Hy = -dt / (mu_yy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 1] += ch_Hy * (dEz_dx_Hy - dEx_dz_Hy)\n\n    # ---- Hz component update ----\n    # Hz: dEx/dy - dEy/dx\n    dEx_dy_Hz = (Ex[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n    dEy_dx_Hz = (Ey[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n\n    ch_Hz = -dt / (mu_zz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 2] += ch_Hz * (dEx_dy_Hz - dEy_dx_Hz)","originalFile":"\"\"\"Core Maxwell timestepping kernels for Phase 1.\n\nThis module provides the GPU-accelerated E and H field update kernels.\nThe kernels follow the Warp backend conventions documented in ``backend.py``.\n\nKernel Organization\n------------------\nThe Maxwell update is split into explicit stages:\n\n1. **boundary_exchange** — fill ghost cells before each update\n2. **electric_update** — update electric field components\n3. **source_injection** — add source contributions to E\n4. **boundary_exchange** — re-exchange E ghosts before H update\n5. **magnetic_update** — update magnetic field components\n6. **pml_stage** — apply PML/ABC damping (post-H update)\n7. **convergence_check** — integrated field intensity (Python-side)\n\nUpdate Equations\n---------------\nFor a Yee lattice with cell sizes ``(dx, dy, dz)`` and timestep ``dt``:\n\nElectric field update::\n\n    Ex[i+1] = Ex[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hz[i,j+1,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dy - (Hy[i,j,k+1] - Hy[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Ey[i+1] = Ey[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hx[i,j,k+1] - Hx[i,j,k])/dz - (Hz[i+1,j,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Ez[i+1] = Ez[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hy[i+1,j,k] - Hy[i,j,k])/dx - (Hx[i,j+1,k] - Hx[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nMagnetic field update::\n\n    Hx[i+1] = Hx[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ez[i,j+1,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dy - (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Hy[i+1] = Hy[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ex[i,j,k+1] - Ex[i,j,k])/dz - (Ez[i+1,j,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Hz[i+1] = Hz[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ey[i+1,j,k] - Ey[i,j,k])/dx - (Ex[i,j+1,k] - Ex[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nCoefficients\n-----------\nFor efficiency, the coefficients are precomputed as::\n\n    ce_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for E update\n    ce_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for H update (note the sign)\n    cm_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for magnetic current (same as ce_e)\n    cm_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for magnetic update (same as ce_h)\n\nIn a homogeneous medium these are constants. In an inhomogeneous medium\nthey vary spatially and are sampled from coefficient arrays.\n\nCapture-Safe Stepping\n---------------------\nAll timestep-dependent values are passed as explicit kernel arguments:\n\n- ``dt``: scalar float timestep\n- ``step_index``: int step counter (for time-varying sources)\n- ``time``: float current simulation time\n\nSource amplitudes are computed in Python and passed as scalar or array\narguments. They are defined using Warp's\n# @wp.kernel decorator for JIT compilation. The actual implementations follow\n# the Yee lattice update equations documented in this module's docstring.\n\nif WARP_AVAILABLE:\n\n    # Component indices for field arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3)\n    _EX, _EY, _EZ = 0, 1, 2\n    _HX, _HY, _HZ = 0, 1, 2\n\n    @wp.kernel\n    def electric_update_3d(\n        E: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        H: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_xx: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_yy: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_zz: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        dt: float,\n        dx: float,\n        dy: float,\n        dz: float,\n        nx: int,\n        ny: int,\n        nz: int,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Update electric field components for 3D Yee lattice.\n\n        This kernel updates Ex, Ey, Ez at interior cells using the\n        curl of the magnetic field. Ghost cells must be filled by\n        boundary exchange before calling this kernel.\n\n        Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n        component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n        Yee lattice curl formulas (Faraday's law, vacuum):\n            Ex: dHz/dy - update interior cells only (skip ghost cells at boundaries)\n        # Valid interior indices: i in [1, nx-2], j in [1, ny-2], k in [1, nz-2]\n        if i < 1 or i >= nx - 1 or j < 1 or j >= ny - 1 or k < 1 or k >= nz - 1:\n            return\n\n        # ---- Ex component update ----\n        # Ex at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHz/dy\n        # and Hy at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHy/dz\n        # dHz_dy = (Hz[i, j+1, k, Hz] - Hz[i, j, k, Hz]) / dy\n        # dHy_dz = (Hy[i, j, k+1, Hy] - Hy[i, j, k, Hy]) / dz\n        Hz_at_ij = H[i, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hz_at_ip1j = H[i, j + 1, k, _HZ]\n        Hy_at_ijk = H[i, j, k, _HY]\n        Hy_at_ijkp1 = H[i, j, k + 1, _HY]\n\n        dHz_dy_Ex = (Hz_at_ip1j - Hz_at_ij) / dy\n        dHy_dz_Ex = (Hy_at_ijkp1 - Hy_at_ijk) / dz\n\n        eps_Ex = eps_xx[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ex = dt / (eps_Ex + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EX] += ce_Ex * (dHz_dy_Ex - dHy_dz_Ex)\n\n        # ---- Ey component update ----\n        # Ey at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHz/dx\n        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHx/dz\n        Hz_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hz_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hx_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n        Hx_at_ijkp1 = H[i, j, k + 1, _HX]\n\n        dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz_at_ip1jk - Hz_at_ijk_Ey) / dx\n        dHx_dz_Ey = (Hx_at_ijkp1 - Hx_at_ijk_Ey) / dz\n\n        eps_Ey = eps_yy[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ey = dt / (eps_Ey + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EY] += ce_Ey * (dHz_dx_Ey - dHx_dz_Ey)\n\n        # ---- Ez component update ----\n        # Ez at (i, j, k) uses Hy at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHy/dx\n        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHx/dy\n        Hy_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HY]\n        Hy_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HY]\n        Hx_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n        Hx_at_ijp1k = H[i, j + 1, k, _HX]\n\n        dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy_at_ip1jk - Hy_at_ijk_Ez) / dx\n        dHx_dy_Ez = (Hx_at_ijp1k - Hx_at_ijk_Ez) / dy\n\n        eps_Ez = eps_zz[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ez = dt / (eps_Ez + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EZ] += ce_Ez * (dHy_dx_Ez - dHx_dy_Ez)\n\n    @wp.kernel\n    def magnetic_update_3d(\n        H: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        E: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_xx: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_yy: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_zz: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        dt: float,\n        dx: float,\n        dy: float,\n        dz: float,\n        nx: int,\n        ny: int,\n        nz: int,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Update magnetic field components for 3D Yee lattice.\n\n        This kernel updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the\n        curl of the electric field. Ghost cells must be filled by\n        boundary exchange before calling this kernel.\n\n        Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n        component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n        Yee lattice curl formulas (Ampere-Maxwell law, vacuum):\n            Hx: dEy/dz - dEz/dy\n            Hy: dEz/dx - dEx/dz\n            Hz: dEx/dy - dEy/dx\n\n        Each component update uses neighbor E values at offset positions\n        corresponding to the Yee cell staggering.\n\n        Args:\n            H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Hx, Hy, Hz)\n            E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Ex, Ey, Ez)\n            mu_xx, mu_yy, mu_zz: Permeability arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz)\n            dt: Timestep size\n            dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes\n            nx, ny, nz: Grid dimensions\n        \"\"\"\n        i, j, k = wp.tid()\n\n        # Bounds check - update interior   dEz_dy_Hx = (Ez_at_ijp1k - Ez_at_ijk_Hx) / dy\n\n        mu_Hx = mu_xx[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hx = -dt / (mu_Hx + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HX] += ch_Hx * (dEy_dz_Hx - dEz_dy_Hx)\n\n        # ---- Hy component update ----\n        # Hy at (i, j, k) uses Ez at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dEz/dx\n        # and Ex at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dEx/dz\n        Ez_at_ijk_Hy = E[i, j, k, _EZ]\n        Ez_at_ip1jk = E[i + 1, j, k, _EZ]\n        Ex_at_ijk_Hy = E[i, j, k, _EX]\n        Ex_at_ijkp1 = E[i, j, k + 1, _EX]\n\n        dEz_dx_Hy = (Ez_at_ip1jk - Ez_at_ijk_Hy) / dx\n        dEx_dz_Hy = (Ex_at_ijkp1 - Ex_at_ijk_Hy) / dz\n\n        mu_Hy = mu_yy[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hy = -dt / (mu_Hy + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HY] += ch_Hy * (dEz_dx_Hy - dEx_dz_Hy)\n\n        # ---- Hz component update ----\n        # Hz at (i, j, k) uses Ex at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dEx/dy\n        # and Ey at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dEy/dx\n        Ex_at_ijk_Hz = E[i, j, k, _EX]\n        Ex_at_ijp1k = E[i, j + 1, k, _EX]\n        Ey_at_ijk_Hz = E[i, j, k, _EY]\n        Ey_at_ip1jk = E[i + 1, j, k, _EY]\n\n        dEx_dy_Hz = (Ex_at_ijp1k - Ex_at_ijk_Hz) / dy\n        dEy_dx_Hz = when Warp is unavailable\n    electric_update_3d = None\n    magnetic_update_3d = None\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Array allocation helpers\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef allocate_maxwell_arrays(\n    grid_shape: tuple[int, int, int],\n    *,\n    complex_policy: ComplexFieldPolicy | a Maxwell stepper.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    grid_shape : tuple[int, int, int]\n        Grid dimensions (Nx, Ny, Nz).\n    complex_policy : ComplexFieldPolicy, optional\n Returns\n    -------\n    dict[str, wp.array | np.ndarray]\n        Dictionary of allocated arrays: E, H, eps_xx, eps_yy, eps_zz,\n        mu_xx, mu_yy, mu_zz.\n    \"\"\"\n    if complex_policy is int = 10,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Execute multiple vacuum Maxwell timesteps with periodic diagnostics.\n\n    This is a convenience runner for vacuum propagation tests that\n    exercises the complete E/H update cycle and collects metrics.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    arrays : dict[str, np.ndarray]\n        Field and coefficient arrays from ``allocate_maxwell_arrays``.\n    dt : float\n        Timestep size.\n    dx, dy, dz : float\n        Cell sizes.\n    num_steps : int, default=100\n        Number of timesteps to execute.\n    interval : int, default=10\n        Interval for collecting diagnostics.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    list[dict[str, Any]]\n        List of diagnostics dicts at each ``interval`` step.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    nx, ny, nz = arrays[\"E\"].shape[:3]\n    num_cells = nx * ny * nz\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        step_result = step_maxwell(\n            arrays,\n            dt,\n            dx,\n            dy,\n            dz,\n            step_index=step,\n            time=step * dt,\n        )\n\n        if step % interval == 0:\n            # Compute integrated electric field energy for diagnostics\n            E = arrays[\"E\"]\n            H = arrays[\"H\"]\n            eps_xx = arrays[\"eps_xx\"]\n            mu_xx = arrays[\"mu_xx\"]\n\n            # E energy = 0.5 * sum(eps * |E|^2) over interior cells\n            i1, i2 = 1, nx - 1\n            j1, j2 = 1, ny - 1\n            k1, k2 = 1, nz - 1\n\n            E_sq = np.sum(E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n            H_sq = np.sum(H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n\n            # Approximate vacuum energy (eps0=1, mu0=1 for normalized)\n            e_energy = 0.5 * np.mean(eps_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * E_sq\n            h_energy = 0.5 * np.mean(mu_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * H_sq\n\n            step_result[\"E_energy\"] = float(e_energy)\n            step_result[\"H_energy\"] = kernels for Phase 1.\n\nThis module provides the GPU-accelerated E and H field update kernels.\nThe kernels follow the Warp backend conventions documented in ``backend.py``.\n\nKernel Organization\n------------------\nThe Maxwell update is split into explicit stages:\n\n1. **boundary_exchange** — fill ghost cells before each update\n2. **electric_update** — update electric field components\n3. **source_injection** — add source contributions to E\n4. **boundary_exchange** — re-exchange E ghosts before H update\n5. **magnetic_update** — update magnetic field components\n6. **pml_stage** — apply PML/ABC damping (post-H update)\n7. **convergence_check** — integrated field intensity (Python-side)\n\nUpdate Equations\n---------------\nFor a Yee lattice with cell sizes ``(dx, dy, dz)`` and timestep ``dt``:\n\nElectric field update::\n\n    Ex[i+1] = Ex[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hz[i,j+1,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dy - (Hy[i,j,k+1] - Hy[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Ey[i+1] = Ey[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hx[i,j,k+1] - Hx[i,j,k])/dz - (Hz[i+1,j,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Ez[i+1] = Ez[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hy[i+1,j,k] - Hy[i,j,k])/dx - (Hx[i,j+1,k] - Hx[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nMagnetic field update::\n\n    Hx[i+1] = Hx[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ez[i,j+1,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dy - (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Hy[i+1] = Ex[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nCoefficients\n-----------\nFor efficiency, the coefficients are precomputed as::\n\n    ce_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for E update\n    ce_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for H update (note the sign)\n    cm_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for magnetic current (same as ce_e)\n    cm_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for magnetic update (same as ce_h)\n\nIn a homogeneous medium these are constants. In an inhomogeneous medium\nthey vary spatially and are sampled from coefficient arrays.\n\nCapture-Safe Stepping\n---------------------\nAll timestep-dependent values are passed as explicit kernel arguments:\n\n- ``dt``: scalar float timestep\n- ``step_index``: int step counter (for time-varying sources)\n- ``time``: float current simulation time\n\nSource amplitudes are computed in Python and passed as scalar or array\narguments. np.ndarray,\n    eps_xx: np.ndarray,\n    eps_yy: np.ndarray,\n    eps_zz: np.ndarray,\n    dt: float,\n    dx: float,\n    dy: float,\n    dz: float,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"NumPy implementation of 3D electric field update.\n\n    Updates Ex, Ey, Ez at interior cells using the curl of H.\n    Ghost cells (index 0 and -1 on each axis) are not updated.\n\n    Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n    component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n    Args:\n        E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        eps_xx, eps_yy, eps_zz: Permittivity arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz).\n        dt: Timestep size.\n        dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes.\n    \"\"\"\n    nx, ny, nz = E.shape[:3]\n    # Interior indices: [1, nx-2], [1, ny-2], [1, nz-2]\n    i1, i2 = 1, nx - 1\n    j1, j2 = 1, ny - 1\n    k1, k2 = 1, nz - 1\n\n    # Precompute 1/dy, 1/dz, 1/dx for efficiency\n    inv_dy = 1.0 / dy\n    inv_dz = 1.0 / dz\n    inv_dx = 1.0 / dx\n\n    # ---- Ex component update ----\n    # Ex: dHz/dy - dHy/dz\n    # dHz_dy = (Hz[:, j+1, :, 2] - Hz[:, j, :, 2]) / dy  at i,j,k\n    # dHy_dz = (Hy[:, :, k+1, 1] - Hy[:, :, k, 1]) / dz  at i,j,k\n    Hz = H[:, :, :, 2]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n    Hy = H[:, :, :, 1]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n\n    dHz_dy_Ex = (Hz[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Hz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n    dHy_dz_Ex = (Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n\n    ce_Ex = dt / (eps_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 0] += ce_Ex * (dHz_dy_Ex - dHy_dz_Ex)\n\n    # ---- Ey component update ----\n    # Ey: dHx/dz - dHz/dx\n    Hx = H[:, :, :, 0]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n\n    dHx_dz_Ey = (Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n    dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n\n    ce_Ey = dt / (eps_yy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 1] += ce_Ey * (dHx_dz_Ey - dHz_dx_Ey)\n\n    # ---- Ez component update ----\n    # Ez: dHy/dx - dHx/dy\n    dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n    dHx_dy_Ez = (Hx[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n\n    ce_Ez = dt / (eps_zz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 2] += ce_Ez * (dHy_dx_Ez - dHx_dy_Ez)\n\n\ndef numpy_magnetic_update_3d(\n    H: np.ndarray,\n    E: np.ndarray,\n    mu_xx: np.ndarray,\n    mu_yy: np.ndarray,\n    mu_zz: np.ndarray,\n    dt: float,\n    dx: float,\n    dy: float,\n    dz: float,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"NumPy implementation of 3D magnetic field update.\n\n    Updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the curl of E.\n    Ghost cells (index 0 and -1 on each axis) are not updated.\n\n    Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n    component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n    Args:\n        H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        mu_xx, mu_yy, mu_zz: Permeability arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz).\n        dt: Timestep 1, nz - 1\n\n    # Precompute 1/dy, 1/dz, 1/dx for efficiency\n    inv_dy = 1.0 / dy\n    inv_dz = 1.0 / dz\n    inv_dx = 1.0 / dx\n\n    # ---- Hx component update ----\n    # Hx: dEy/dz - dEz/dy\n Hz[i,j,k])/dy - (Hy[i,j,k+1] - Hy[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Ey[i+1] = Ey[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hx[i,j,k+1] - Hx[i,j,k])/dz - (Hz[i+1,j,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Ez[i+1] = Ez[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hy[i+1,j,k] - Hy[i,j,k])/dx - (Hx[i,j+1,k] - Hx[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nMagnetic field update::\n\n    Hx[i+1] = Hx[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ez[i,j+1,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dy - (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Hy[i+1] = Hy[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ex[i,j,k+1] - Ex[i,j,k])/dz - (Ez[i+1,j,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Hz[i+1] = Hz[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ey[i+1,j,k] - Ey[i,j,k])/dx - (Ex[i,j+1,k] - Ex[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nCoefficients\n-----------\nFor efficiency, the coefficients are precomputed as::\n\n    ce_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for E update\n    ce_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for H update (note the sign)\n    cm_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for magnetic current (same as ce_e)\n    cm_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for magnetic update (same as ce_h)\n\nIn a homogeneous medium these are constants. In an inhomogeneous medium\nthey vary spatially and are sampled from coefficient arrays.\n\nCapture-Safe Stepping\n---------------------\nAll timestep-dependent values are passed as explicit kernel arguments:\n\n- ``dt``: scalar float timestep\n- ``step_index``: int step counter (for time-varying sources)\n- ``time``: float current simulation time\n\nSource amplitudes are computed in Python and passed as scalar or array\narguments. No closure captures are used in kernel definitions.\n\nReferences\n---------\n- Vacuum update: ``../meep/src/update_eh.cpp``\n- Kernel formula sources: ``../phase1/kernel-formula-sources.md``\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any, Literal\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom autofdtd.kernels.backend import (\n    WARP_AVAILABLE,\n    ComplexFieldPolicy,\n    WarpTimer,\n    allocate_coefficient_array,\n    allocate_field_array,\n    backend_info,\n    nvtx_range,\n    step_metrics,\n    wp,\n)\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"electric_update_3d\",\n    \"magnetic_update_3d\",\n    \"MaxwellStepKernelSpec\",\n    \"allocate_maxwell_arrays\",\n    \"step_maxwell\",\n    \"step_kernel_metadata\",\n]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kernel metadata\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass MaxwellStepKernelSpec:\n    \"\"\"Metadata for a Maxwell timestep kernel.\n\n    Attributes\n    ----------\n    name : str\n        Kernel function name.\n    field_family : str\n        \"electric\" or \"magnetic\".\n    uses_pml : bool\n        Whether this kernel requires PML coefficient arrays.\n    uses_sources : bool\n        Whether this kernel has source injection integrated.\n    supports_complex : bool\n        Whether this kernel supports complex-valued fields.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str\n    field_family: Literal[\"electric\", \"magnetic\"]\n    uses_pml: bool = False\n    uses_sources: bool = False\n    supports_complex: bool = True\n\n\ndef step_kernel_metadata() -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Expose the current stepping kernel metadata for diagnostics.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"backend\": \"warp\" if WARP_AVAILABLE else \"numpy\",\n        \"warp_available\": WARP_AVAILABLE,\n        \"module_contents_stable\": False,  # Set True after kernels are finalized\n        \"electric_update\": MaxwellStepKernelSpec(\n            name=\"electric_update_3d\",\n            field_family=\"electric\",\n            uses_pml=False,\n            uses_sources=False,\n            supports_complex=True,\n        ),\n        \"magnetic_update\": MaxwellStepKernelSpec(\n            name=\"magnetic_update_3d\",\n            field_family=\"magnetic\",\n            uses_pml=False,\n            uses_sources=False,\n            supports_complex=True,\n        ),\n        \"update_stages\": (\n            \"boundary_exchange\",\n            \"electric_update\",\n            \"source_injection\",\n            \"boundary_exchange\",\n            \"magnetic_update\",\n            \"pml_stage\",\n            \"monitor_collection\",\n            \"convergence_check\",\n        ),\n        \"curl_component_mapping\": {\n            \"Ex\": (\"Hz\", \"dy\", \"Hy\", \"dz\"),  # (curl_component, cofactor1, neighbor_field, cofactor2)\n            \"Ey\": (\"Hz\", \"dz\", \"Hx\", \"dx\"),\n            \"Ez\": (\"Hy\", \"dx\", \"Hx\", \"dy\"),\n            \"Hx\": (\"Ez\", \"dy\", \"Ey\", \"dz\"),\n            \"Hy\": (\"Ez\", \"dz\", \"Ex\", \"dx\"),\n            \"Hz\": (\"Ey\", \"dx\", \"Ex\", \"dy\"),\n        },\n        \"coefficient_requires\": (\"eps_xx\", \"eps_yy\", \"eps_zz\", \"mu_xx\", \"mu_yy\", \"mu_zz\"),\n    }\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Warp kernel definitions\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# These are the core Maxwell update kernels. They are defined using Warp's\n# @wp.kernel decorator for JIT compilation. The actual implementations follow\n# the Yee lattice update equations documented in this module's docstring.\n\nif WARP_AVAILABLE:\n\n    # Component indices for field arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3)\n    _EX, _EY, _EZ = 0, 1, 2\n    _HX, _HY, _HZ = 0, 1, 2\n\n    @wp.kernel\n    def electric_update_3d(\n        E: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        H: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_xx: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_yy: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_zz: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        dt: float,\n        dx: float,\n        dy: float,\n        dz: float,\n        nx: int,\n        ny: int,\n        nz: int,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Update electric field components for 3D Yee lattice.\n\n        This kernel updates Ex, Ey, Ez at interior cells using the\n        curl of the magnetic field. Ghost cells must be filled by\n        boundary exchange before calling this kernel.\n\n        Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n        component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n        Yee lattice curl formulas (Faraday's law, vacuum):\n            Ex: dHz/dy - dHy/dz\n            Ey: dHx/dz - dHz/dx\n            Ez: dHy/dx - dHx/dy\n\n        Each component update uses neighbor H values at offset positions\n        corresponding to the Yee cell staggering.\n\n        Args:\n            E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Ex, Ey, Ez)\n            H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Hx, Hy, Hz)\n            eps_xx, eps_yy, eps_zz: Permittivity arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz)\n            dt: Timestep size\n            dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes\n            nx, ny, nz: Grid dimensions\n        \"\"\"\n        i, j, k = wp.tid()\n\n        # Bounds check - update interior cells only (skip ghost cells at boundaries)\n        # Valid interior indices: i in [1, nx-2], j in [1, ny-2], k in [1, nz-2]\n        if i < 1 or i >= nx - 1 or j < 1 or j >= ny - 1 or k < 1 or k >= nz - 1:\n            return\n\n        # ---- Ex component update ----\n        # Ex at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHz/dy\n        # and Hy at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHy/dz\n        # dHz_dy = (Hz[i, j+1, k, Hz] - Hz[i, j, k, Hz]) / dy\n        # dHy_dz = (Hy[i, j, k+1, Hy] - Hy[i, j, k, Hy]) / dz\n        Hz_at_ij = H[i, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hz_at_ip1j = H[i, j + 1, k, _HZ]\n        Hy_at_ijk = H[i, j, k, _HY]\n        Hy_at_ijkp1 = H[i, j, k + 1, _HY]\n\n        dHz_dy_Ex = (Hz_at_ip1j - Hz_at_ij) / dy\n        dHy_dz_Ex = (Hy_at_ijkp1 - Hy_at_ijk) / dz\n\n        eps_Ex = eps_xx[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ex = dt / (eps_Ex + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EX] += ce_Ex * (dHz_dy_Ex - dHy_dz_Ex)\n\n        # ---- Ey component update ----\n        # Ey at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHz/dx\n        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHx/dz\n        Hz_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hz_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hx_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n        Hx_at_ijkp1 = H[i, j, k + 1, _HX]\n\n        dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz_at_ip1jk - Hz_at_ijk_Ey) / dx\n        dHx_dz_Ey = (Hx_at_ijkp1 - Hx_at_ijk_Ey) / dz\n\n        eps_Ey = eps_yy[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ey = dt / (eps_Ey + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EY] += ce_Ey * (dHz_dx_Ey - dHx_dz_Ey)\n\n        # ---- Ez component update ----\n        # Ez at (i, j, k) uses Hy at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHy/dx\n        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHx/dy\n        Hy_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HY]\n        Hy_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HY]\n        Hx_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n        Hx_at_ijp1k = H[i, j + 1, k, _HX]\n\n        dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy_at_ip1jk - Hy_at_ijk_Ez) / dx\n        dHx_dy_Ez = (Hx_at_ijp1k - Hx_at_ijk_Ez) / dy\n\n        eps_Ez = eps_zz[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ez = dt / (eps_Ez + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EZ] += ce_Ez * (dHy_dx_Ez - dHx_dy_Ez)\n\n    @wp.kernel\n    def magnetic_update_3d(\n        H: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        E: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_xx: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_yy: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_zz: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        dt: float,\n        dx: float,\n        dy: float,\n        dz: float,\n        nx: int,\n        ny: int,\n        nz: int,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Update magnetic field components for 3D Yee lattice.\n\n        This kernel updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the\n        curl of the electric field. Ghost cells must be filled by\n        boundary exchange before calling this kernel.\n\n        Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n        component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n        Yee lattice curl formulas (Ampere-Maxwell law, vacuum):\n            Hx: dEy/dz - dEz/dy\n            Hy: dEz/dx - dEx/dz\n            Hz: dEx/dy - dEy/dx\n\n        Each component update uses neighbor E values at offset positions\n        corresponding to the Yee cell staggering.\n\n        Args:\n            H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Hx, Hy, Hz)\n            E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Ex, Ey, Ez)\n            mu_xx, mu_yy, mu_zz: Permeability arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz)\n            dt: Timestep size\n            dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes\n            nx, ny, nz: Grid dimensions\n        \"\"\"\n        i, j, k = wp.tid()\n\n        # Bounds check - update interior cells only (skip ghost cells at boundaries)\n        if i < 1 or i >= nx - 1 or j < 1 or j >= ny - 1 or k < 1 or k >= nz - 1:\n            return\n\n        # ---- Hx component update ----\n        # Hx at (i, j, k) uses Ey at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dEy/dz\n        # and Ez at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dEz/dy\n        # Hx_update = -dt/mu * (dEy_dz - dEz_dy)\n        Ey_at_ijk_Hx = E[i, j, k, _EY]\n        Ey_at_ijkp1 = E[i, j, k + 1, _EY]\n        Ez_at_ijk_Hx = E[i, j, k, _EZ]\n        Ez_at_ijp1k = E[i, j + 1, k, _EZ]\n\n        dEy_dz_Hx = (Ey_at_ijkp1 - Ey_at_ijk_Hx) / dz\n        dEz_dy_Hx = (Ez_at_ijp1k - Ez_at_ijk_Hx) / dy\n\n        mu_Hx = mu_xx[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hx = -dt / (mu_Hx + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HX] += ch_Hx * (dEy_dz_Hx - dEz_dy_Hx)\n\n        # ---- Hy component update ----\n        # Hy at (i, j, k) uses Ez at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dEz/dx\n        # and Ex at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dEx/dz\n        Ez_at_ijk_Hy = E[i, j, k, _EZ]\n        Ez_at_ip1jk = E[i + 1, j, k, _EZ]\n        Ex_at_ijk_Hy = E[i, j, k, _EX]\n        Ex_at_ijkp1 = E[i, j, k + 1, _EX]\n\n        dEz_dx_Hy = (Ez_at_ip1jk - Ez_at_ijk_Hy) / dx\n        dEx_dz_Hy = (Ex_at_ijkp1 - Ex_at_ijk_Hy) / dz\n\n        mu_Hy = mu_yy[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hy = -dt / (mu_Hy + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HY] += ch_Hy * (dEz_dx_Hy - dEx_dz_Hy)\n\n        # ---- Hz component update ----\n        # Hz at (i, j, k) uses Ex at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dEx/dy\n        # and Ey at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dEy/dx\n        Ex_at_ijk_Hz = E[i, j, k, _EX]\n        Ex_at_ijp1k = E[i, j + 1, k, _EX]\n        Ey_at_ijk_Hz = E[i, j, k, _EY]\n        Ey_at_ip1jk = E[i + 1, j, k, _EY]\n\n        dEx_dy_Hz = (Ex_at_ijp1k - Ex_at_ijk_Hz) / dy\n        dEy_dx_Hz = (Ey_at_ip1jk - Ey_at_ijk_Hz) / dx\n\n        mu_Hz = mu_zz[i, j, k]\n        ch_Hz = -dt / (mu_Hz + 1e-20)\n        H[i, j, k, _HZ] += ch_Hz * (dEx_dy_Hz - dEy_dx_Hz)\n\nelse:\n    # Stub definitions when Warp is unavailable\n    electric_update_3d = None\n    magnetic_update_3d = None\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# NumPy fallback implementations (for CPU execution without Warp)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef numpy_electric_update_3d(\n    E: np.ndarray,\n    H: np.ndarray,\n    eps_xx: np.ndarray,\n    eps_yy: np.ndarray,\n    eps_zz: np.ndarray,\n    dt: float,\n    dx: float,\n    dy: float,\n    dz: float,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"NumPy implementation of 3D electric field update.\n\n    Updates Ex, Ey, Ez at interior cells using the curl of H.\n    Ghost cells (index 0 and -1 on each axis) are not updated.\n\n    Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n    component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n    Args:\n        E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        eps_xx, eps_yy, eps_zz: Permittivity arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz).\n        dt: Timestep size.\n        dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes.\n    \"\"\"\n    nx, ny, nz = E.shape[:3]\n    # Interior indices: [1, nx-2], [1, ny-2], [1, nz-2]\n    i1, i2 = 1, nx - 1\n    j1, j2 = 1, ny - 1\n    k1, k2 = 1, nz - 1\n\n    # Precompute 1/dy, 1/dz, 1/dx for efficiency\n    inv_dy = 1.0 / dy\n    inv_dz = 1.0 / dz\n    inv_dx = 1.0 / dx\n\n    # ---- Ex component update ----\n    # Ex: dHz/dy - dHy/dz\n    # dHz_dy = (Hz[:, j+1, :, 2] - Hz[:, j, :, 2]) / dy  at i,j,k\n    # dHy_dz = (Hy[:, :, k+1, 1] - Hy[:, :, k, 1]) / dz  at i,j,k\n    Hz = H[:, :, :, 2]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n    Hy = H[:, :, :, 1]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n\n    dHz_dy_Ex = (Hz[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Hz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n    dHy_dz_Ex = (Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n\n    ce_Ex = dt / (eps_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 0] += ce_Ex * (dHz_dy_Ex - dHy_dz_Ex)\n\n    # ---- Ey component update ----\n    # Ey: dHx/dz - dHz/dx\n    Hx = H[:, :, :, 0]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n\n    dHx_dz_Ey = (Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n    dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n\n    ce_Ey = dt / (eps_yy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 1] += ce_Ey * (dHx_dz_Ey - dHz_dx_Ey)\n\n    # ---- Ez component update ----\n    # Ez: dHy/dx - dHx/dy\n    dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n    dHx_dy_Ez = (Hx[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n\n    ce_Ez = dt / (eps_zz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 2] += ce_Ez * (dHy_dx_Ez - dHx_dy_Ez)\n\n\ndef numpy_magnetic_update_3d(\n    H: np.ndarray,\n    E: np.ndarray,\n    mu_xx: np.ndarray,\n    mu_yy: np.ndarray,\n    mu_zz: np.ndarray,\n    dt: float,\n    dx: float,\n    dy: float,\n    dz: float,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"NumPy implementation of 3D magnetic field update.\n\n    Updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the curl of E.\n    Ghost cells (index 0 and -1 on each axis) are not updated.\n\n    Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n    component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n    Args:\n        H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n        mu_xx, mu_yy, mu_zz: Permeability arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz).\n        dt: Timestep size.\n        dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes.\n    \"\"\"\n    nx, ny, nz = H.shape[:3]\n    # Interior indices: [1, nx-2], [1, ny-2], [1, nz-2]\n    i1, i2 = 1, nx - 1\n    j1, j2 = 1, ny - 1\n    k1, k2 = 1, nz - 1\n\n    # Precompute 1/dy, 1/dz, 1/dx for efficiency\n    inv_dy = 1.0 / dy\n    inv_dz = 1.0 / dz\n    inv_dx = 1.0 / dx\n\n    # ---- Hx component update ----\n    # Hx: dEy/dz - dEz/dy\n    Ey = E[:, :, :, 1]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n    Ez = E[:, :, :, 2]\n\n    dEy_dz_Hx = (Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n    dEz_dy_Hx = (Ez[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Ez[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n\n    ch_Hx = -dt / (mu_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 0] += ch_Hx * (dEy_dz_Hx - dEz_dy_Hx)\n\n    # ---- Hy component update ----\n    # Hy: dEz/dx - dEx/dz\n    Ex = E[:, :, :, 0]\n\n    dEz_dx_Hy = (Ez[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Ez[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n    dEx_dz_Hy = (Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n\n    ch_Hy = -dt / (mu_yy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 1] += ch_Hy * (dEz_dx_Hy - dEx_dz_Hy)\n\n    # ---- Hz component update ----\n    # Hz: dEx/dy - dEy/dx\n    dEx_dy_Hz = (Ex[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n    dEy_dx_Hz = (Ey[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n\n    ch_Hz = -dt / (mu_zz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n    H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 2] += ch_Hz * (dEx_dy_Hz - dEy_dx_Hz)\n\n\ndef allocate_maxwell_arrays(\n    grid_shape: tuple[int, int, int],\n    *,\n    complex_policy: ComplexFieldPolicy | None = None,\n    device: int | None = None,\n    chunk_index: tuple[int, int, int] | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, \"wp.array | np.ndarray\"]:\n    \"\"\"Allocate     assert result[\"backend\"] == \"numpy\"\n\n    def test_vacuum_propagation_is_energy_conserving(self):\n        \"\"\"Vacuum propagation should approximately conserve total energy.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a plane wave pulse\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n\n        # Set a Gaussian pulse in Ez\n        sigma = 3\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)  # Ez component\n\n        # Record initial energy\n        i1, i2 = 1, self.nx - 1\n        j1, j2 = 1, self.ny - 1\n        k1, k2 = 1, self.nz - 1\n\n        E0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        H0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        initial_energy = 0.5 * (E0_sq + H0_sq)\n\n        # Run several steps\n        for step in range(50):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        # Compute final energy\n        Ef_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        Hf_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        final_energy = 0.5 * (Ef_sq + Hf_sq)\n\n        # Energy should be approximately conserved (within ~10% for this coarse test)\n        if initial_energy > 1e-10:\n            relative_change = abs(final_energy - initial_energy) / initial_energy\n test_vacuum_wave_speed_correct(self):\n        \"\"\"A Gaussian pulse in vacuum should propagate at speed c.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a narrow Ez pulse at the center\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n        sigma = 2\n\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)\n\n        # Record initial peak position\n        def peak_position(E):\n            \"\"\"Find weighted centroid of |E|^2.\"\"\"\n            E_mag = np.sum(E**2, axis=-1)  # sum over components\n            total = np.sum(E_mag) + 1e-20\n            cx_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nx)[:, None, None]) / total\n            cy_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.ny)[None, :, None]) / total\n            cz_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nz)[None, None, :]) / total\n            return cx_e, cy_e, cz_e\n\n        initial_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Run enough steps for pulse to propagate\n        c = 3e8\n        cells_per_step = c * self.dt / self.dx  # should be < 1 for CFL\n        num_steps = 30\n\n        for step in range(num_steps):\n time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        final_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Peak should have moved in z direction (where Ez pulse is)\n        dz_expected = order as expected\n        assert abs(dz_observed) > 0.5 * abs(dz_expected), (\n            f\"Wave appears to have moved {dz_observed*1e9:.2f} nm, \"\n            f\"expected ~{dz_expected*1e9:.2f} nm\"\n        )\n\n    def test_vacuum_maxwell_step_returns_diagnostics(self):\n        \"\"\"vacuum_maxwell_step returns list of diagnostics.\"\"\"\n        results = vacuum_maxwell_step(\n So the difference between consecutive slices should be zero, which means dEy_dz_Hx is zero, and therefore H doesn't get updated. If Ey is uniform, then dEy_dz should be zero everywhere, so Hx shouldn't change. That's the bug - I'm not accounting for the case where E is constant in space.\n\n```\ndEy_dz_Hx = (Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Ey[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n```\n\nIf Ey = 1 everywhere, then the difference is 0, so dEy_dz_Hx = 0, and Hx remains unchanged.\n\nThis suggests either the test itself is flawed, or there's a fundamental issue with how I'm computing the curl. But in my implementation, I'm using the same Ey and Ez arrays for all H component calculations, which suggests I'm missing the staggered positioning. When I compute dEy/dz = (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k]) / dz, I'm actually calculating the derivative across two full cells rather than the half-cell offset that the Yee lattice requires.\n\nThe issue becomes clear when I consider a plane wave propagating in vacuum—with uniform Ey everywhere, dEy/dz should indeed be zero, so Hx shouldn't change. But the test expects it to, which means my formulation is wrong. The curl of E should produce the correct H field behavior for a traveling wave, not leave it zero.\n\nFor a z-propagating plane wave with only Ey present, the spatial derivative dEy/dz should be non-zero and proportional to the temporal derivative of Hx according to Maxwell's equations. This means my curl calculation isn't capturing the wave physics correctly—it's treating uniform fields as physically meaningful when they're not. Hx depends on dEy/dz, which is zero for a uniform Ey field, so this particular test case won't produce a field update. I need to design a test where Ey actually varies with k to generate a non-zero derivative.\n\nLet me reconsider the update formula: Hx[i,j,k] depends on (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k])/dz, which requires Ey to change across the k dimension. If Ey is constant everywhere, there's no curl to drive Hx. My test needs to create that spatial variation in Ey to actually see the field change.\n\nFor a plane wave propagating in the z-direction with Ey as the field component, the field varies with both z and time. But I should think about what static field configuration would produce a curl in Hx. Maybe I need to reconsider what \"non-zero E\" means for this particular H update—it might require a different field configuration than just setting Ey = 1 everywhere.\n\nThe real issue is that in vacuum FDTD with no H field initially, the H update depends on the curl of E. If E is spatially uniform, there's no curl, so H won't change. So my test won't actually validate the H update formula itself. But this is actually correct physics—a uniform E field in vacuum shouldn't generate any H field through Faraday's law.\n\nI need to either set up a non-uniform E field to actually produce a curl, or test the H update with E already having some curl, or verify that the update doesn't change H when curl(E) = 0. self.arrays[\"mu_xx\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"mu_yy\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"mu_zz\"].fill(1.0)\n\n    def test_electric_update_preserves_zero_fields(self):\n        \"\"\"Zero E field remains zero after update with zero H.\"\"\"\n        E_before = self.arrays[\"E\"].copy()\n        numpy_electric_update_3d(\n            self.arrays[\"E\"],\n            self.arrays[\"H\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_xx\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_yy\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_zz\"],\n            self.dt,\n            self.dx,\n            self.dy,\n            self.dz,\n        )\n        # Should be unchanged (still zero)\n        np.testing.assert_allclose(self.arrays[\"E\"], E_before, atol=1e-15)\n\n    def test_electric_update_changes_with_nonzero_H(self):\n        \"\"\"Non-zero H field causes E field to change.\"\"\"\n        # Set a uniform H field in Hz component\n        self.arrays[\"H\"][:, :, :, 2] = 1.0  # Hz = 1\n\n        E_before = self.arrays[\"E\"].copy()\n        numpy_electric_update_3d(\n            self.arrays[\"E\"],\n            self.arrays[\"H\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_xx\"],\n    assert np.linalg.norm(self.arrays[\"E\"]) > 0.0\n\n    def test_electric_update_ex_component(self):\n        \"\"\"Ex component update uses dHz/dy - dHy/dz.\"\"\"\n        # Set uniform Hy and Hz to isolate Ex update\n        self.arrays[\"H\"][:, :, :, 1] = 0.0  # Hy = 0\n        self.arrays[\"H\"][:, :, :, 2] = 1.0  # Hz = 1\n\n        E_before = self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 0].copy()\n\n        numpy_electric_update_3d(\n            self.arrays[\"E\"],\n            self.arrays[\"H\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_xx\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_yy\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_zz\"],\n            self.dt,\n            self.dx,\n            self.dy,\n            self.dz,\n        )\n\n        # Ex should have changed due to dHz/dy term\n        # With uniform Hz and zero Hy, only dHz/dy contributes\n        dEx = self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 0] - E_before\n        # Interior cells should show non-zero change\n        interior = dEx[1:-1, 1:-1, 1:-1]\n        assert np.max(np.abs(interior)) > 0.0\n\n    def test_electric_update_interior_only(self):\n        \"\"\"Electric update only affects interior cells.\"\"\"\n        # Set non-zero H E_boundary_end_before)\n\n\nclass TestNumpyMagneticUpdate:\n    \"\"\"Test NumPy magnetic field update implementation.\"\"\"\n\n    def setup_method(self):\n        \"\"\"Set up test grid and step (E then H) in vacuum.\"\"\"\n\n    def setup_method(self):\n        \"\"\"Set up vacuum simulation.\"\"\"\n        self.nx, self.ny, self.nz = 20, 20, 20\n        self.dx = self.dy = self.dz = 1e-8\n        # CFL-safe timestep\n        c = 3e8\n        self.dt = 0.99 * min(self.dx, self.dy, self.dz) / c\n\n        self.arrays = allocate_maxwell_arrays(\n            (self.nx, self.ny, self.nz), complex_policy=ComplexFieldPolicy()\n        )\n\n        for key in self.arrays:\n            self.arrays[key].fill(0.0)\n\n        self.arrays[\"eps_xx\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"eps_yy\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"eps_zz\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"mu_xx\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"mu_yy\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"mu_zz\"].fill(1.0)\n\n    def test_step_maxwell_numpy_returns_metrics(self):\n        \"\"\"step_maxwell returns structured metrics.\"\"\"\n        result = step_maxwell(\n            self.arrays, self.dt, self.dx, self.dy, self.dz, step_index=0\n        )\n        assert \"step_index\" in result\n        assert \"cells_updated\" in result\n        assert \"backend\" in result\n        assert result[\"backend\"] == \"numpy\"\n\n    def test_vacuum_propagation_is_energy_conserving(self):\n        \"\"\"Vacuum propagation should approximately conserve total energy.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a plane wave pulse\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n\n        # Set a Gaussian pulse in Ez\n        sigma = 3\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)  # Ez component\n\n        # Record initial energy\n        i1, i2 = 1, self.nx - 1\n        j1, j2 = 1, self.ny - 1\n        k1, k2 = 1, self.nz - 1\n\n        E0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        H0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        initial_energy = 0.5 * (E0_sq + H0_sq)\n\n        # Run several steps\n        for step in range(50):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        # Compute final energy\n        Ef_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        Hf_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        final_energy = 0.5 * (Ef_sq + Hf_sq)\n\n        # Energy should be approximately conserved (within ~10% for this coarse test)\n        if initial_energy > 1e-10:\n            relative_change = abs(final_energy - initial_energy) / initial_energy\n            assert relative_change < 0.15, (\n                f\"Energy changed by {relative_change*100:.1f}%, \"\n                f\"expected < 15% for vacuum propagation\"\n            )\n\n    def test_vacuum_wave_speed_correct(self):\n        \"\"\"A Gaussian pulse in vacuum should propagate at speed c.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a narrow Ez pulse at the center\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n        sigma = 2\n\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)\n\n        # Record initial peak position\n        def peak_position(E):\n            \"\"\"Find weighted centroid of |E|^2.\"\"\"\n            E_mag = np.sum(E**2, axis=-1)  # sum over components\n            total = np.sum(E_mag) + 1e-20\n            cx_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nx)[:, None, None]) / total\n            cy_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.ny)[None, :, None]) / total\n            cz_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nz)[None, None, :]) / total\n            return cx_e, cy_e, cz_e\n\n        initial_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Run enough steps for pulse to propagate\n        c = 3e8\n        cells_per_step = c * self.dt / self.dx  # should be < 1 for CFL\n        num_steps = 30\n\n        for step in range(num_steps):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        final_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Peak should have moved in z direction (where Ez pulse is)\n        dz_expected = num_steps * cells_per_step * self.dx\n        dz_observed = (final_pos[2] - initial_pos[2]) * self.dx\n\n        # Allow for numerical dispersion and grid effects\n        # Observed should be same order as expected\n        assert abs(dz_observed) > 0.5 * abs(dz_expected), (\n            f\"Wave appears to have moved {dz_observed*1e9:.2f} nm, \"\n            f\"expected ~{dz_expected*1e9:.2f} nm\"\n        )\n\n    def test_vacuum_maxwell_step_returns_diagnostics(self):\n        \"\"\"vacuum_maxwell_step returns list of diagnostics.\"\"\"\n        results = vacuum_maxwell_step(\n            self.arrays,\n            self.dt,\n            self.dx,\n            self.dy,\n            self.dz,\n            num_steps=20,\n            interval=10,\n        )\n\n        assert len(results) == z\n\n        E_before = self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 0].copy()\n\n        numpy_electric_update_3d(\n            self.arrays[\"E\"],\n            self.arrays[\"H\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_xx\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_yy\"],\n            self.arrays[\"eps_zz\"],\n            self.dt,\n            self.dx,\n            self.dy,\n            self.dz,\n        )\n\n        # Ex should have changed due to dHz/dy and dHy/dz terms\n        dEx = self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 0] - E_before\n        # Interior cells should show non-zero change\n        interior = dEx[1:-1, 1:-1, 1:-1]\n        assert np.max(np.abs(interior)) > 0.0\n\n    def test_electric_update_interior_only(self):\n        \"\"\"Electric update only affects interior cells.\"\"\"\n        #     assert result[\"backend\"] == \"numpy\"\n\n    def test_vacuum_propagation_is_energy_conserving(self):\n        \"\"\"Vacuum propagation should approximately conserve total energy.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a plane wave pulse\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n\n        # Set a Gaussian pulse in Ez\n        sigma = 3\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)  # Ez component\n\n        # Record initial energy\n        i1, i2 = 1, self.nx - 1\n        j1, j2 = 1, self.ny - 1\n        k1, k2 = 1, self.nz - 1\n\n        E0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        H0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        initial_energy = 0.5 * (E0_sq + H0_sq)\n\n        # Run several steps\n        for step in range(50):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        # Compute final energy\n        Ef_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        Hf_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        final_energy = 0.5 * (Ef_sq + Hf_sq)\n\n        # Energy should be approximately conserved (within ~10% for this coarse test)\n        if initial_energy > 1e-10:\n            relative_change = abs(final_energy - initial_energy) / initial_energy\n test_vacuum_wave_speed_correct(self):\n        \"\"\"A Gaussian pulse in vacuum should propagate at speed c.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a narrow Ez pulse at the center\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n        sigma = 2\n\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)\n\n        # Record initial peak position\n        def peak_position(E):\n            \"\"\"Find weighted centroid of |E|^2.\"\"\"\n            E_mag = np.sum(E**2, axis=-1)  # sum over components\n            total = np.sum(E_mag) + 1e-20\n            cx_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nx)[:, None, None]) / total\n            cy_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.ny)[None, :, None]) / total\n            cz_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nz)[None, None, :]) / total\n            return cx_e, cy_e, cz_e\n\n        initial_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Run enough steps for time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        final_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Peak should have moved in z direction (where Ez pulse is)\n        dz_expected = test_vacuum_propagation_is_energy_conserving(self):\n        \"\"\"Vacuum propagation should approximately conserve total energy.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a plane wave pulse\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n\n        # Set a Gaussian pulse in Ez\n        sigma = 3\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)  # Ez component\n\n        # Record initial energy\n        i1, i2 = 1, self.nx - 1\n        j1, j2 = 1, self.ny - 1\n        k1, k2 = 1, self.nz - 1\n\n        E0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        H0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        initial_energy = 0.5 * (E0_sq + H0_sq)\n\n        # Run several steps\n        for step in range(50):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        # Compute final energy\n        Ef_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        Hf_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        final_energy = 0.5 * (Ef_sq + Hf_sq)\n\n        # Energy should be approximately conserved (within ~10% for this coarse test)\n        if initial_energy > 1e-10:\n            relative_change = abs(final_energy - initial_energy) / initial_energy\n            assert relative_change < 0.15, (\n                f\"Energy changed by {relative_change*100:.1f}%, \"\n                f\"expected < 15% for vacuum propagation\"\n            )\n\n    def test_vacuum_wave_speed_correct(self):\n        \"\"\"A Gaussian pulse in vacuum should propagate at speed c.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a narrow Ez pulse at the center\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n        sigma = 2\n\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)\n\n        # Record initial peak position\n        def peak_position(E):\n            \"\"\"Find weighted centroid of |E|^2.\"\"\"\n            E_mag = np.sum(E**2, axis=-1)  # sum over components\n            total = np.sum(E_mag) + 1e-20\n            cx_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nx)[:, None, None]) / total\n            cy_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.ny)[None, :, None]) / total\n            cz_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nz)[None, None, :]) / total\n            return cx_e, cy_e, cz_e\n\n        initial_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Run enough steps for pulse to propagate\n        c = 3e8\n        cells_per_step = c * self.dt / self.dx  # should be < 1 for CFL\n        num_steps = 30\n\n        for step in range(num_steps):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        final_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Peak should have moved in z direction (where Ez pulse is)\n        dz_expected = num_steps * cells_per_step * self.dx\n        dz_observed = (final_pos[2] - initial_pos[2]) * self.dx\n\n        # Allow for numerical dispersion and grid effects\n        # Observed should be same order as expected\n        assert abs(dz_observed) > 0.5 * abs(dz_expected), (\n            f\"Wave appears to have moved {dz_observed*1e9:.2f} nm, \"\n            f\"expected ~{dz_expected*1e9:.2f} nm\"\n        )\n\n    def test_vacuum_maxwell_step_returns_diagnostics(self):\n        \"\"\"vacuum_maxwell_step returns list of diagnostics.\"\"\"\n        results = vacuum_maxwell_step(\n            self.arrays,\n            self.dt,\n            self.dx,\n            self.dy,\n            self.dz,\n            num_steps=20,\n            interval=10,\n        )\n\n        assert len(results) == 3  # step 0, 10, 20\n\n      self.nx, self.ny, self.nz = 20, 20, 20\n        self.dx = self.dy = self.dz = 1e-8\n        # CFL-safe timestep\n        c = 3e8\n        self.dt = 0.99 * min(self.dx, self.dy, self.dz) / c\n\n        self.arrays = allocate_maxwell_arrays(\n            (self.nx, self.ny, self.nz), complex_policy=ComplexFieldPolicy()\n        )\n\n        for key in self.arrays:\n            self.arrays[key].fill(0.0)\n\n        self.arrays[\"eps_xx\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"eps_yy\"].fill(1.0)\n        self.arrays[\"eps_zz\"].fill(1.0)\n \"step_index\" in result\n        assert \"cells_updated\" in result\n        assert \"backend\" in result\n        assert result[\"backend\"] == \"numpy\"\n\n    def test_vacuum_propagation_is_energy_conserving(self):\n        \"\"\"Vacuum propagation should approximately conserve total energy.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a plane wave pulse\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n\n        # Set a Gaussian pulse in Ez\n        sigma = 3\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)  # Ez component\n\n        # Record initial energy\n        i1, i2 = 1, self.nx - 1\n        j1, j2 = 1, self.ny - 1\n        k1, k2 = 1, self.nz - 1\n\n        E0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        H0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        initial_energy = 0.5 * (E0_sq + H0_sq)\n\n        # Run several steps\n        for step in range(50):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        # Compute final energy\n        Ef_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        Hf_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        final_energy = 0.5 * (Ef_sq + Hf_sq)\n\n        # Energy should be approximately conserved (within ~10% for this coarse test)\n        if initial_energy > 1e-10:\n            relative_change = abs(final_energy - initial_energy) / initial_energy\n            assert relative_change < 0.15, (\n                f\"Energy changed by {relative_change*100:.1f}%, \"\n                f\"expected < 15% for vacuum propagation\"\n            )\n\n    def test_vacuum_wave_speed_correct(self):\n        \"\"\"A Gaussian pulse in vacuum should propagate at speed c.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a narrow Ez pulse at the center\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n        sigma = 2\n\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)\n\n        # Record initial peak position\n        def peak_position(E):\n            \"\"\"Find weighted centroid of |E|^2.\"\"\"\n            E_mag = np.sum(E**2, axis=-1)  # sum over components\n            total = np.sum(E_mag) + 1e-20\n            cx_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nx)[:, None, None]) / total\n            cy_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.ny)[None, :, None]) / total\n            cz_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nz)[None, None, :]) / total\n            return cx_e, cy_e, cz_e\n\n        initial_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Run enough steps for pulse to propagate\n        c = 3e8\n        cells_per_step = c * self.dt / self.dx  # should be < 1 for CFL\n        num_steps = 30\n\n        for step in range(num_steps):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        final_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Peak should have moved in z direction (where Ez pulse is)\n        dz_expected = num_steps * cells_per_step * self.dx\n        dz_observed = (final_pos[2] - initial_pos[2]) * self.dx\n\n        # Allow for numerical dispersion and grid effects\n        # Observed should be same order as expected\n        assert abs(dz_observed) > 0.5 * abs(dz_expected), (\n            f\"Wave appears to have moved {dz_observed*1e9:.2f} nm, \"\n            f\"expected ~{dz_expected*1e9:.2f} nm\"\n        )\n\n    def test_vacuum_maxwell_step_returns_diagnostics(self):\n        \"\"\"vacuum_maxwell_step returns list of diagnostics.\"\"\"\n        results = vacuum_maxwell_step(\n            self.arrays,\n            self.dt,\n            self.dx,\n            self.dy,\n            self.dz,\n            num_steps=20,\n            interval=10,\n        )\n\n        assert len(results) == 3  # step 0, 10, 20\n\n numpy_magnetic_update_3d(\n            self.arrays[\"H\"],\n            self.arrays[\"E\"],\n            self.arrays[\"mu_xx\"],\n            self.arrays[\"mu_yy\"],\n            self.arrays[\"mu_zz\"],\n            self.dt,\n            self.dx,\n            self.dy,\n            self.dz,\n        )\n\n        np.testing.assert_allclose(self.arrays[\"H\"][0, :, :, :], H_boundary_before)\n\n\nclass TestVacuumMaxwellStep:\n    \"\"\"Test complete Maxwell step (E then H) in vacuum.\"\"\"\n\n    def setup_method(self):\n        \"\"\"Set up vacuum simulation.\"\"\"\n        self.nx, self.ny, self.nz = 20, 20, 20\n        self.dx = self.dy = self.dz = 1e-8\n        # CFL-safe timestep\n        c = 3e8\n        self.dt = 0.99 * min(self.dx, self.dy, self.dz) / c\n\n \"\"\"step_maxwell returns structured metrics.\"\"\"\n        result = step_maxwell(\n            self.arrays, self.dt, self.dx, self.dy, self.dz, step_index=0\n        )\n        assert \"step_index\" in result\n        assert \"cells_updated\" in result\n        assert \"backend\" in result\n        assert result[\"backend\"] == \"numpy\"\n\n    def test_vacuum_propagation_is_energy_conserving(self):\n        \"\"\"Vacuum propagation should approximately conserve total energy.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a plane wave pulse\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n\n        # Set a Gaussian pulse in Ez\n        sigma = 3\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)  # Ez component\n\n        # Record initial energy\n        i1, i2 = 1, self.nx - 1\n        j1, j2 = 1, self.ny - 1\n        k1, k2 = 1, self.nz - 1\n\n        E0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        H0_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        initial_energy = 0.5 * (E0_sq + H0_sq)\n\n        # Run several steps\n        for step in range(50):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        # Compute final energy\n        Ef_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"E\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        Hf_sq = np.sum(self.arrays[\"H\"][i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n        final_energy = 0.5 * (Ef_sq + Hf_sq)\n\n        # Energy should be approximately conserved (within ~10% for this coarse test)\n        if initial_energy > 1e-10:\n            relative_change = abs(final_energy - initial_energy) / initial_energy\n            assert relative_change < 0.15, (\n                f\"Energy changed by {relative_change*100:.1f}%, \"\n                f\"expected < 15% for vacuum propagation\"\n            )\n\n    def test_vacuum_wave_speed_correct(self):\n        \"\"\"A Gaussian pulse in vacuum should propagate at speed c.\"\"\"\n        # Initialize a narrow Ez pulse at the center\n        cx, cy, cz = self.nx // 2, self.ny // 2, self.nz // 2\n        sigma = 2\n\n        for i in range(self.nx):\n            for j in range(self.ny):\n                for k in range(self.nz):\n                    r2 = ((i - cx) ** 2 + (j - cy) ** 2 + (k - cz) ** 2) / (\n                        2 * sigma**2\n                    )\n                    self.arrays[\"E\"][i, j, k, 2] = np.exp(-r2)\n\n        # Record initial peak position\n        def peak_position(E):\n            \"\"\"Find weighted centroid of |E|^2.\"\"\"\n            E_mag = np.sum(E**2, axis=-1)  # sum over components\n            total = np.sum(E_mag) + 1e-20\n            cx_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nx)[:, None, None]) / total\n            cy_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.ny)[None, :, None]) / total\n            cz_e = np.sum(E_mag * np.arange(self.nz)[None, None, :]) / total\n            return cx_e, cy_e, cz_e\n\n        initial_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Run enough steps for pulse to propagate\n        c = 3e8\n        cells_per_step = c * self.dt / self.dx  # should be < 1 for CFL\n        num_steps = 30\n\n        for step in range(num_steps):\n            step_maxwell(\n                self.arrays,\n                self.dt,\n                self.dx,\n                self.dy,\n                self.dz,\n                step_index=step,\n                time=step * self.dt,\n            )\n\n        final_pos = peak_position(self.arrays[\"E\"][:, :, :, 2])\n\n        # Peak should have moved in z direction (where Ez pulse is)\n        dz_expected = num_steps * cells_per_step * self.dx\n        dz_observed = (final_pos[2] - initial_pos[2]) * self.dx\n\n        # Allow for numerical dispersion and grid effects\n        # Observed should be same order as expected\n        assert abs(dz_observed) > 0.5 * abs(dz_expected), (\n            f\"Wave appears to have moved {dz_observed*1e9:.2f} nm, \"\n            f\"expected ~{dz_expected*1e9:.2f} nm\"\n        )\n\n    def test_vacuum_maxwell_step_returns_diagnostics(self):\n        \"\"\"vacuum_maxwell_step returns list of diagnostics.\"\"\"\n        results = vacuum_maxwell_step(\n            self.arrays,\n
