{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_function_lapv1gli56od_1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Core Maxwell timestepping kernels for Phase 1.\n2\t\n3\tThis module provides the GPU-accelerated E and H field update kernels.\n4\tThe kernels follow the Warp backend conventions documented in ``backend.py``.\n5\t\n6\tKernel Organization\n7\t------------------\n8\tThe Maxwell update is split into explicit stages:\n9\t\n10\t1. **boundary_exchange** — fill ghost cells before each update\n11\t2. **electric_update** — update electric field components\n12\t3. **source_injection** — add source contributions to E\n13\t4. **boundary_exchange** — re-exchange E ghosts before H update\n14\t5. **magnetic_update** — update magnetic field components\n15\t6. **pml_stage** — apply PML/ABC damping (post-H update)\n16\t7. **convergence_check** — integrated field intensity (Python-side)\n17\t\n18\tUpdate Equations\n19\t---------------\n20\tFor a Yee lattice with cell sizes ``(dx, dy, dz)`` and timestep ``dt``:\n21\t\n22\tElectric field update::\n23\t\n24\t    Ex[i+1] = Ex[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n25\t        (Hz[i,j+1,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dy - (Hy[i,j,k+1] - Hy[i,j,k])/dz\n26\t    )\n27\t    Ey[i+1] = Ey[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n28\t        (Hx[i,j,k+1] - Hx[i,j,k])/dz - (Hz[i+1,j,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dx\n29\t    )\n30\t    Ez[i+1] = Ez[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n31\t        (Hy[i+1,j,k] - Hy[i,j,k])/dx - (Hx[i,j+1,k] - Hx[i,j,k])/dy\n32\t    )\n33\t\n34\tMagnetic field update::\n35\t\n36\t    Hx[i+1] = Hx[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n37\t        (Ez[i,j+1,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dy - (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k])/dz\n38\t    )\n39\t    Hy[i+1] = Hy[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n40\t        (Ex[i,j,k+1] - Ex[i,j,k])/dz - (Ez[i+1,j,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dx\n41\t    )\n42\t    Hz[i+1] = Hz[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n43\t        (Ey[i+1,j,k] - Ey[i,j,k])/dx - (Ex[i,j+1,k] - Ex[i,j,k])/dy\n44\t    )\n45\t\n46\tCoefficients\n47\t-----------\n48\tFor efficiency, the coefficients are precomputed as::\n49\t\n50\t    ce_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for E update\n51\t    ce_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for H update (note the sign)\n52\t    cm_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for magnetic current (same as ce_e)\n53\t    cm_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for magnetic update (same as ce_h)\n54\t\n55\tIn a homogeneous medium these are constants. In an inhomogeneous medium\n56\tthey vary spatially and are sampled from coefficient arrays.\n57\t\n58\tCapture-Safe Stepping\n59\t---------------------\n60\tAll timestep-dependent values are passed as explicit kernel arguments:\n61\t\n62\t- ``dt``: scalar float timestep\n63\t- ``step_index``: int step counter (for time-varying sources)\n64\t- ``time``: float current simulation time\n65\t\n66\tSource amplitudes are computed in Python and passed as scalar or array\n67\targuments. No closure captures are used in kernel definitions.\n68\t\n69\tReferences\n70\t---------\n71\t- Vacuum update: ``../meep/src/update_eh.cpp``\n72\t- Kernel formula sources: ``../phase1/kernel-formula-sources.md``\n73\t\"\"\"\n74\t\n75\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n76\t\n77\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n78\tfrom typing import Any, Literal\n79\t\n80\timport dHy/dz\n219\t            Ey: dHx/dz - dHz/dx\n220\t            Ez: dHy/dx - dHx/dy\n221\t\n222\t        Each component update uses neighbor H values at offset positions\n223\t        corresponding to the Yee cell staggering.\n224\t\n225\t        Args:\n226\t            E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Ex, Ey, Ez)\n227\t            H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Hx, Hy, Hz)\n228\t            eps_xx, eps_yy, eps_zz: Permittivity arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz)\n229\t            dt: Timestep size\n230\t            dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes\n231\t            nx, ny, nz: Grid dimensions\n232\t        \"\"\"\n233\t        i, j, k = wp.tid()\n234\t\n235\t        # Bounds check - 1 or k < 1 or k >= nz - 1:\n238\t            return\n239\t\n240\t        # ---- Ex component update ----\n241\t        # Ex at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHz/dy\n242\t        # and Hy at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHy/dz\n243\t        # dHz_dy = (Hz[i, j+1, k, Hz] - Hz_at_ij) / dy\n251\t        dHy_dz_Ex = (Hy_at_ijkp1 - Hy_at_ijk) / dz\n252\t\n253\t        eps_Ex = eps_xx[i, j, k]\n254\t        ce_Ex = dt / (eps_Ex + 1e-20)\n255\t        E[i, j, k, _EX] += ce_Ex * (dHz_dy_Ex - dHy_dz_Ex)\n256\t\n257\t        # ---- Ey component update ----\n258\t        # Ey at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHz/dx\n259\t        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHx/dz\n260\t        Hz_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HZ]\n261\t        Hz_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HZ]\n262\t        Hx_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n263\t        Hx_at_ijkp1 = H[i, j, k + 1, _HX]\n264\t\n265\t        dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz_at_ip1jk - dHx_dz_Ey)\n271\t\n272\t        # ---- Ez component update ----\n273\t        # Ez at (i, j, k) uses Hy at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHy/dx\n274\t        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHx/dy\n275\t        Hy_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HY]\n276\t        Hy_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HY]\n277\t        Hx_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n278\t        Hx_at_ijp1k = H[i, j + 1, k, _HX]\n279\t\n280\t        dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy_at_ip1jk - dHx_dy_Ez)\n286\t\n287\t    @wp.kernel\n288\t    def magnetic_update_3d(\n289\t        H: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n290\t        E: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n291\t        mu_xx: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n292\t        mu_yy: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n293\t        mu_zz: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n294\t        dt: float,\n295\t        dx: float,\n296\t        dy: float,\n297\t        dz: float,\n298\t        nx: int,\n299\t        ny: int,\n300\t        nz: int,\n301\t    ):\n302\t        \"\"\"Update magnetic field components for 3D Yee lattice.\n303\t\n304\t        This kernel updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the\n305\t        curl of the electric field. dEx/dz\n314\t            Hz: dEx/dy - dEy/dx\n315\t\n316\t        Each component update uses neighbor E values at offset positions\n317\t        corresponding to the Yee cell staggering.\n318\t\n319\t Ey_at_ijkp1 = E[i, j, k + 1, _EY]\n339\t        Ez_at_ijk_Hx = E[i, j, k, _EZ]\n340\t        Ez_at_ijp1k = E[i, j + 1, k, _EZ]\n341\t\n342\t        dEy_dz_Hx = (Ey_at_ijkp1 - Ey_at_ijk_Hx) / dz\n343\t dEz_dy_Hx)\n348\t\n349\t        # ---- Hy component update ----\n350\t        # Hy at (i, j, k) uses Ez at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dEz/dx\n351\t        # and Ex at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n386\t# NumPy fallback implementations (for CPU execution without Warp)\n387\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n388\t\n389\t\n390\tdef numpy_electric_update_3d(\n391\t    E: np.ndarray,\n392\t    H: np.ndarray,\n393\t    eps_xx: np.ndarray,\n394\t    eps_yy: np.ndarray,\n395\t    eps_zz: np.ndarray,\n396\t    dt: float,\n397\t    dx: float,\n398\t    dy: float,\n399\t    dz: float,\n400\t) -> None:\n401\t    \"\"\"NumPy implementation of 3D electric field update.\n402\t\n403\t    Updates Ex, Ey, Ez at interior cells using the curl of H.\n404\t    Ghost cells (index 0 and -1 on each axis) are not updated.\n405\t\n406\t    Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n407\t    component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n408\t\n409\t    Args:\n410\t        E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n411\t        H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3).\n412\t        eps_xx, eps_yy, eps_zz: Permittivity arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz).\n413\t        dt: Timestep size.\n414\t        dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes.\n415\t    \"\"\"\n416\t    nx, ny, nz = E.shape[:3]\n417\t    # Interior indices: [1, nx-2], [1, ny-2], [1, nz-2]\n418\t    i1, i2 = 1, nx - 1\n419\t    j1, j2 = 1, ny - 1\n420\t    k1, k2 = 1, nz - 1\n421\t\n422\t    # Precompute 1/dy, 1/dz, 1/dx for efficiency\n423\t    inv_dy = 1.0 / dy\n424\t    inv_dz = 1.0 / dz\n425\t    inv_dx = 1.0 / dx\n426\t\n427\t    # ---- Ex component update ----\n428\t    # Ex: dHz/dy - dHy/dz\n429\t    # dHz_dy = (Hz[:, j+1, :, 2] - Hz[:, j, :, 2]) / dy  at i,j,k\n430\t    # (Hz[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Hz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n435\t    dHy_dz_Ex = (Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n436\t\n437\t    ce_Ex = dt / (eps_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n438\t    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 0] += ce_Ex * (dHz_dy_Ex - dHy_dz_Ex)\n439\t\n440\t    # ---- Ey component update ----\n441\t    # Ey: dHx/dz - dHz/dx\n442\t    Hx = H[:, :, :, 0]  # shape (nx, ny, nz)\n443\t\n444\t    dHx_dz_Ey = (Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n445\t    dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n446\t\n447\t    ce_Ey = dt / (eps_yy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n448\t    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 1] += ce_Ey * (dHx_dz_Ey - dHz_dx_Ey)\n449\t\n450\t    # ---- Ez component update ----\n451\t    # Ez: dHy/dx - dHx/dy\n452\t    dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy[i1+1:i2+1, j1:j2, k1:k2] - Hy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dx\n453\t    dHx_dy_Ez = (Hx[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Hx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n454\t\n455\t    ce_Ez = dt / (eps_zz[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n456\t    E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 2] += ce_Ez * (dHy_dx_Ez - dHx_dy_Ez)\n457\t\n458\t\n459\tdef numpy_magnetic_update_3d(\n460\t    H: np.ndarray,\n461\t    E: np.ndarray,\n462\t    mu_xx: np.ndarray,\n463\t    mu_yy: np.ndarray,\n464\t    mu_zz: np.ndarray,\n465\t    dt: float,\n466\t    dx: float,\n467\t    dy: float,\n468\t    dz: float,\n469\t) -> None:\n470\t    \"\"\"NumPy implementation of 3D magnetic field update.\n471\t\n472\t    Updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the curl of E.\n473\t    Ghost cells (index 0 and -1 on each axis) are not updated.\n474\t\n475\t    Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n476\t    component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and dEx_dz_Hy = (Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1+1:k2+1] - Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dz\n513\t\n514\t    ch_Hy = -dt / (mu_yy[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2] + 1e-20)\n515\t    H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, 1] += ch_Hy * (dEz_dx_Hy - dEx_dz_Hy)\n516\t\n517\t    # ---- Hz component update ----\n518\t    # Hz: dEx/dy - dEy/dx\n519\t    dEx_dy_Hz = (Ex[i1:i2, j1+1:j2+1, k1:k2] - Ex[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * inv_dy\n520\t (dEx_dy_Hz - dEy_dx_Hz)\n524\t\n525\t\n526\tdef allocate_maxwell_arrays(\n527\t    grid_shape: tuple[int, int, int],\n528\t    *,\n529\t    complex_policy: ComplexFieldPolicy | None = None,\n530\t )\n701\t\n702\t        step_end = time_module.perf_counter()\n703\t        wall_time = step_end - step_start\n704\t\n705\t        metrics = step_metrics(\n706\t list[dict[str, Any]]:\n789\t    \"\"\"Execute multiple vacuum Maxwell timesteps with periodic diagnostics.\n790\t\n791\t    This is a convenience runner for vacuum propagation tests that\n792\t    exercises the complete E/H update cycle and collects metrics.\n793\t\n794\t    Parameters\n795\t    ----------\n796\t    arrays : dict[str, np.ndarray]\n797\t        Field and coefficient arrays from List of diagnostics dicts at each ``interval`` step.\n811\t    \"\"\"\n812\t    results = []\n813\t    nx, ny, nz = arrays[\"E\"].shape[:3]\n814\t    num_cells = nx * ny * nz\n815\t\n816\t    for step step_index=step,\n824\t            time=step * dt,\n825\t        )\n826\t\n827\t        if step % interval == 0:\n828\t            # Compute integrated electric field energy for diagnostics\n829\t            E = arrays[\"E\"]\n830\t            H = arrays[\"H\"]\n831\t            eps_xx = arrays[\"eps_xx\"]\n832\t            mu_xx = arrays[\"mu_xx\"]\n833\t\n834\t            # E energy = 0.5 * j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n840\t            H_sq = np.sum(H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n841\t\n842\t            # Approximate vacuum energy (eps0=1, mu0=1 for normalized)\n843\t            e_energy = results.append(step_result)\n850\t\n851\t    return results\n852\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b12143ec-27d0-4fe6-a81a-c82ccae4f4e4","uuid":"1ddb731c-3771-421d-9df2-efc961d6cf8c","timestamp":"2026-04-07T09:07:37.170Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/workspace/autofdtd/src/autofdtd/kernels/steps.py","content":"\"\"\"Core Maxwell timestepping kernels for Phase 1.\n\nThis module provides the GPU-accelerated E and H field update kernels.\nThe kernels follow the Warp backend conventions documented in ``backend.py``.\n\nKernel Organization\n------------------\nThe Maxwell update is split into explicit stages:\n\n1. **boundary_exchange** — fill ghost cells before each update\n2. **electric_update** — update electric field components\n3. **source_injection** — add source contributions to E\n4. **boundary_exchange** — re-exchange E ghosts before H update\n5. **magnetic_update** — update magnetic field components\n6. **pml_stage** — apply PML/ABC damping (post-H update)\n7. **convergence_check** — integrated field intensity (Python-side)\n\nUpdate Equations\n---------------\nFor a Yee lattice with cell sizes ``(dx, dy, dz)`` and timestep ``dt``:\n\nElectric field update::\n\n    Ex[i+1] = Ex[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hz[i,j+1,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dy - (Hy[i,j,k+1] - Hy[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Ey[i+1] = Ey[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hx[i,j,k+1] - Hx[i,j,k])/dz - (Hz[i+1,j,k] - Hz[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Ez[i+1] = Ez[i] + dt/(eps[i]*eps0) * (\n        (Hy[i+1,j,k] - Hy[i,j,k])/dx - (Hx[i,j+1,k] - Hx[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nMagnetic field update::\n\n    Hx[i+1] = Hx[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ez[i,j+1,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dy - (Ey[i,j,k+1] - Ey[i,j,k])/dz\n    )\n    Hy[i+1] = Hy[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ex[i,j,k+1] - Ex[i,j,k])/dz - (Ez[i+1,j,k] - Ez[i,j,k])/dx\n    )\n    Hz[i+1] = Hz[i] - dt/(mu[i]*mu0) * (\n        (Ey[i+1,j,k] - Ey[i,j,k])/dx - (Ex[i,j+1,k] - Ex[i,j,k])/dy\n    )\n\nCoefficients\n-----------\nFor efficiency, the coefficients are precomputed as::\n\n    ce_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for E update\n    ce_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for H update (note the sign)\n    cm_e = dt / (eps * eps0)    # for magnetic current (same as ce_e)\n    cm_h = -dt / (mu * mu0)     # for magnetic update (same as ce_h)\n\nIn a homogeneous medium these are constants. In an inhomogeneous medium\nthey vary spatially and are sampled from coefficient arrays.\n\nCapture-Safe Stepping\n---------------------\nAll timestep-dependent values are passed as explicit kernel arguments:\n\n- ``dt``: scalar float timestep\n- ``step_index``: int step counter (for time-varying sources)\n- ``time``: float current simulation time\n\nSource amplitudes are computed in Python and passed as scalar or array\narguments. No closure captures are used in kernel definitions.\n\nReferences\n---------\n- Vacuum update: ``../meep/src/update_eh.cpp``\n- Kernel formula sources: ``../phase1/kernel-formula-sources.md``\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any, Literal\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom autofdtd.kernels.backend import (\n    WARP_AVAILABLE,\n    ComplexFieldPolicy,\n    WarpTimer,\n    allocate_coefficient_array,\n    allocate_field_array,\n    backend_info,\n    nvtx_range,\n    step_metrics,\n    wp,\n)\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"electric_update_3d\",\n    \"magnetic_update_3d\",\n    \"numpy_electric_update_3d\",\n    \"numpy_magnetic_update_3d\",\n    \"MaxwellStepKernelSpec\",\n    \"allocate_maxwell_arrays\",\n    \"step_maxwell\",\n    \"vacuum_maxwell_step\",\n    \"step_kernel_metadata\",\n]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Kernel metadata\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass MaxwellStepKernelSpec:\n    \"\"\"Metadata for a Maxwell timestep kernel.\n\n    Attributes\n    ----------\n    name : str\n        Kernel function name.\n    field_family : str\n        \"electric\" or \"magnetic\".\n    uses_pml : bool\n        Whether this kernel requires PML coefficient arrays.\n    uses_sources : bool\n        Whether this kernel has source injection integrated.\n    supports_complex : bool\n        Whether this kernel supports complex-valued fields.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str\n    field_family: Literal[\"electric\", \"magnetic\"]\n    uses_pml: bool = False\n    uses_sources: bool = False\n    supports_complex: bool = True\n\n\ndef step_kernel_metadata() -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Expose the current stepping kernel metadata for diagnostics.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"backend\": \"warp\" if WARP_AVAILABLE else \"numpy\",\n        \"warp_available\": WARP_AVAILABLE,\n        \"module_contents_stable\": False,  # Set True after kernels are finalized\n        \"electric_update\": MaxwellStepKernelSpec(\n \"magnetic_update\": MaxwellStepKernelSpec(\n            name=\"magnetic_update_3d\",\n            field_family=\"magnetic\",\n            uses_pml=False,\n            uses_sources=False,\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Warp kernel definitions\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# These are the core Maxwell update kernels. They are defined using Warp's\n# @wp.kernel decorator for JIT compilation. The actual implementations follow\n# the Yee lattice update equations documented in this module's docstring.\n\nif WARP_AVAILABLE:\n\n    # Component indices for field arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3)\n    _EX, _EY, _EZ = 0, 1, 2\n    _HX, _HY, _HZ = 0, 1, 2\n\n    @wp.kernel\n    def electric_update_3d(\n        E: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        H: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_xx: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_yy: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        eps_zz: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        dt: float,\n        dx: float,\n        dy: float,\n        dz: float,\n        nx: int,\n        ny: int,\n        nz: int,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Update electric field components for 3D Yee lattice.\n\n        This kernel updates Ex, Ey, Ez at interior cells using the\n        curl of the magnetic field. Ghost cells must be filled by\n        boundary exchange before calling this kernel.\n\n        Array layout: E and H have shape (nx, ny, nz, 3) with\n        component order (Ex=0, Ey=1, Ez=2) for E and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n        Yee lattice curl formulas (Faraday's law, vacuum):\n            Ex: dHz/dy - dHy/dz\n            Ey: dHx/dz - dHz/dx\n            Ez: dHy/dx - dHx/dy\n\n        Each component update uses neighbor H values at offset positions\n        corresponding to the Yee cell staggering.\n\n        Args:\n            E: Electric field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Ex, Ey, Ez)\n            H: Magnetic field array with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3), component order (Hx, Hy, Hz)\n            eps_xx, eps_yy, eps_zz: Permittivity arrays with shape (nx, ny, nz)\n            dt: Timestep size\n            dx, dy, dz: Cell sizes\n            nx, ny, nz: Grid dimensions\n        \"\"\"\n        i, j, k = wp.tid()\n\n        # Bounds check - update interior cells only (skip ghost cells at boundaries)\n        # Valid interior indices: i in [1, nx-2], j in [1, ny-2], k in [1, nz-2]\n        if i < 1 or i >= nx - 1 or j < 1 or j >= ny - 1 or k < 1 or k >= nz - 1:\n            return\n\n        # ---- Ex component update ----\n        # Ex at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHz/dy\n        # and Hy at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHy/dz\n        # dHz_dy = (Hz[i, j+1, k, Hz] - Hz_at_ij) / dy\n        dHy_dz_Ex = (Hy_at_ijkp1 - Hy_at_ijk) / dz\n\n        eps_Ex = eps_xx[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ex = dt / (eps_Ex + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EX] += ce_Ex * (dHz_dy_Ex - dHy_dz_Ex)\n\n        # ---- Ey component update ----\n        # Ey at (i, j, k) uses Hz at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHz/dx\n        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j, k+1) for dHx/dz\n        Hz_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hz_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HZ]\n        Hx_at_ijk_Ey = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n        Hx_at_ijkp1 = H[i, j, k + 1, _HX]\n\n        dHz_dx_Ey = (Hz_at_ip1jk - Hz_at_ijk_Ey) / dx\n        dHx_dz_Ey = (Hx_at_ijkp1 - Hx_at_ijk_Ey) / dz\n\n        eps_Ey = eps_yy[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ey = dt / (eps_Ey + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EY] += ce_Ey * (dHz_dx_Ey - dHx_dz_Ey)\n\n        # ---- Ez component update ----\n        # Ez at (i, j, k) uses Hy at (i, j, k) and (i+1, j, k) for dHy/dx\n        # and Hx at (i, j, k) and (i, j+1, k) for dHx/dy\n        Hy_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HY]\n        Hy_at_ip1jk = H[i + 1, j, k, _HY]\n        Hx_at_ijk_Ez = H[i, j, k, _HX]\n        Hx_at_ijp1k = H[i, j + 1, k, _HX]\n\n        dHy_dx_Ez = (Hy_at_ip1jk - Hy_at_ijk_Ez) / dx\n        dHx_dy_Ez = (Hx_at_ijp1k - Hx_at_ijk_Ez) / dy\n\n        eps_Ez = eps_zz[i, j, k]\n        ce_Ez = dt / (eps_Ez + 1e-20)\n        E[i, j, k, _EZ] += ce_Ez * (dHy_dx_Ez - dHx_dy_Ez)\n\n    @wp.kernel\n    def magnetic_update_3d(\n        H: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        E: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_xx: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_yy: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        mu_zz: wp.array(dtype=wp.float32),\n        dt: float,\n        dx: float,\n        dy: float,\n        dz: float,\n        nx: int,\n        ny: int,\n        nz: int,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Update magnetic field components for 3D Yee lattice.\n\n        This kernel updates Hx, Hy, Hz at interior cells using the\n        curl of the electric field. and (Hx=0, Hy=1, Hz=2) for H.\n\n        Yee lattice curl formulas (Ampere-Maxwell law, vacuum):\n            Hx: dEy/dz - dEz/dy\n            Hy: dEz/dx - dEx/dz\n            Hz: dEx/dy - dEy/dx\n\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stepping helper\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef step_maxwell(\n \"warp\",\n    }\n\n\ndef vacuum_maxwell_step(\n    arrays: dict[str, \"np.ndarray\"],\n    dt: float,\n    dx: float,\n    dy: float,\n    dz: float,\n    *,\n    num_steps: int = 100,\n    interval: int = 10,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Execute multiple vacuum Maxwell timesteps with periodic diagnostics.\n\n    This is a convenience runner for vacuum propagation tests that\n    exercises the complete E/H update cycle and collects metrics.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    arrays : dict[str, np.ndarray]\n        Field and coefficient arrays from ``allocate_maxwell_arrays``.\n    dt : float\n        Timestep size.\n    dx, dy, dz : float\n        Cell sizes.\n    num_steps : int, default=100\n        Number of timesteps to execute.\n    interval : int, default=10\n        Interval for collecting diagnostics.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    list[dict[str, Any]]\n        List of diagnostics dicts at each ``interval`` step.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    nx, ny, nz = arrays[\"E\"].shape[:3]\n    num_cells = nx * ny * nz\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        step_result = step_maxwell(\n            arrays,\n            dt,\n            dx,\n            dy,\n            dz,\n            step_index=step,\n            time=step * dt,\n        )\n\n        if step % interval == 0:\n            # Compute integrated electric field energy for diagnostics\n            E = arrays[\"E\"]\n            H = arrays[\"H\"]\n            eps_xx = arrays[\"eps_xx\"]\n            mu_xx = arrays[\"mu_xx\"]\n\n            # E energy = 0.5 * sum(eps * |E|^2) over interior cells\n            i1, i2 = 1, nx - 1\n            j1, j2 = 1, ny - 1\n            k1, k2 = 1, nz - 1\n\n            E_sq = np.sum(E[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n            H_sq = np.sum(H[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2, :] ** 2)\n\n            # Approximate vacuum energy (eps0=1, mu0=1 for normalized)\n            e_energy = 0.5 * np.mean(eps_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * E_sq\n            h_energy = 0.5 * np.mean(mu_xx[i1:i2, j1:j2, k1:k2]) * H_sq\n\n            step_result[\"E_energy\"] = float(e_energy)\n            step_result[\"H_energy\"] = float(h_energy)\n            step_result[\"total_energy\"] = float(e_energy + h_energy)\n            results.append(step_result)\n\n    return results\n","numLines":852,"startLine":1,"totalLines":852}}}
